<p>This is a reimagined version of the Tampa City Council agenda. It removes legalese from the descriptions, parses Background details from the Summary Sheet when available, and links to supporting documents. Also included is a zoning map with current applications. Document links point to our mirrored copies for long-term stability. For original documents, refer to the official final agenda from the clerk in Onbase.</p>

<h2 id="consent-agenda">Consent Agenda</h2>
<h3 class="agenda__committee" id="public-safety-committee">Public Safety Committee</h3>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-27763">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-27763">7</a> File No. PS26-27763</h3>
<p>Resolution of the City of Tampa, Florida approving Quote Number 11327142 under an existing Purchase Agreement between the City of Tampa and Stryker Sales, LLC for the furnishing of Labor and Travel for Power-Pro ProCare Repair Services in the estimated amount of $20,000 over a 36-month term for use by the Tampa Fire and Rescue Department; authorizing the Mayor to execute said Quote.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-27763">
<h4 id="financial-27763" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">This Resolution approves Quote Number 11327142 under the terms of the Purchase Agreement between the City of Tampa and Stryker Sales, LLC for the furnishing of Power- PRO ProCare Repair Services in the estimated amount of $20,000 over a 36-month term for use by the Tampa Fire and Rescue Department.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Total dollars this resolution authorizes the City to spend in the current fiscal year. Inter-fund transfers are excluded to avoid double-counting."><dt>Spending authorized (FY27)</dt><dd>$8,000</dd></div></dl>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__future">Plus <strong>$12,000</strong> in future-year amounts subject to annual Council approval.</p>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$8,000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">General Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Fire Department EMS</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Repair and Maintenance Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY27 · 01100.214700.546000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Expenditure</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure is-future"><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$8,000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">General Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Fire Department EMS</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Repair and Maintenance Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY28 · 01100.214700.546000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Expenditure</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__subject" title="Council is not committing this fiscal year today; subject to future-year appropriation.">subject to annual appropriation</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure is-future"><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$4,000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">General Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Fire Department EMS</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Repair and Maintenance Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY29 · 01100.214700.546000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Expenditure</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__subject" title="Council is not committing this fiscal year today; subject to future-year appropriation.">subject to annual appropriation</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>Tampa Fire Rescue has standardized the purchase of parts, preventative maintenance, and capital equipment from Stryker Sales, LLC. Standardization to this equipment and these services ensures interoperability, maintenance efficiency, continuity of service, and reduced maintenance costs while avoiding redundancy and additional expenditures associated with implementing and maintaining alternative equipment and services.</p>
<p>This amendment will add Power-PRO ProCare services for travel and labor to the existing standardization. The addition of these services will provide the necessary travel and labor support associated with the maintenance and servicing of Tampa Fire Rescue’s Stryker equipment. The amendment will increase the existing award by approximately $20,000 to accommodate these additional services. It is recommended that the City amend the existing award with Stryker Sales, LLC to include Power-PRO ProCare travel and labor services.</p>
<p>Equal Business Opportunity (EBO) Statement: There is no EBO participation associated with this purchase, as the procurement is being made under a standardization.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/27763/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/27763/26-x-00312_stryker_power-pro_procare_repair_services_backup.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">26-X-00312_STRYKER_POWER-PRO_PROCARE_REPAIR_SERVICES_BACKUP.PDF</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/27763/26-x-00312_stryker_power-pro_procare_repair_services_reso_agreement.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">26-X-00312_STRYKER_POWER-PRO_PROCARE_REPAIR_SERVICES_RESO_AGREEMENT.PDF</a></li>
</ul>
</div>
</article>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-27867">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-27867">8</a> File No. PS26-27867</h3>
<p>Resolution approving the acceptance of a Voluntary Cooperation Mutual Aid Agreement between the City of Tampa on behalf of the Tampa Police Department (&quot;TPD&quot;), and the Florida Department of Law Enforcement (&quot;FDLE&quot;) for the Electronic Surveillance Support Team (&quot;ESST&quot;) Multi-Agency Voluntary Cooperation Mutual Aid Agreement.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>By authority contained in Resolution No. 2025-590, passed and adopted by City Council of Tampa, Florida on July 17, 2025, the City of Tampa on behalf of the Tampa Police Department approved the execution of the Interlocal Agreement for the Tampa Bay Regional Counter-Unmanned Arial Systems (c-UAS) Initiatives.  The Tampa Bay Regional c-UAS Work Group was formed and is operating in a multi-agency effort under the FDLE ESST Task Force to collaborate efforts, joint funding and the sharing of specialized c-UAS equipment and trained personnel.  TPD desires to participate as an agency party in the FDLE ESST Task Force to join the coordinated regional response to protect public safety from from the increased threat posed by unauthorized or malicious use of Unmanned Aerial Systems (“UAS” or “drones”).  The subscribing law enforcement agencies are so located in relation to each other that it is to the advantage of each to receive and extend mutual aid in the form of law enforcement services and resources to adequately respond to continuing, multi-jurisdictional criminal activity, so as to protect the public peace and safety, and preserve the lives and property of the citizens.  The respective law enforcement agencies have the authority under Part 1, Chapter 23, Florida Statutes, “the Florida Mutual Aid Act,” to enter into a voluntary agreement for cooperation and assistance of a routine law enforcement nature that crosses jurisdictional lines.  Tampa Police Department participation will benefit the citizens of the City of Tampa.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/27867/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/27867/reso-approve-voluntary-cooperation-mutual-aid-agmt-sep15.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">RESO APPROVE VOLUNTARY COOPERATION MUTUAL AID AGMT (SEP15).PDF</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/27867/reso-2025-590-pasco-drone-sharing-ila-cuas.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">RESO# 2025-590 PASCO DRONE SHARING ILA CUAS.PDF</a></li>
</ul>
</div>
</article>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-27940">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-27940">9</a> File No. PS26-27940</h3>
<p>Resolution approving Service Schedule No. 5 and Quote between Versaterm Public Safety US, Inc., (&quot;Versaterm&quot;) and the City of Tampa relating to the use of OpsIntel Software Application (&quot;Software&quot;) from July 1, 2026 through June 30, 2027 in an amount of $152,364.59 as part of the UASI grant funding Resolution No. 2026-383.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-27940">
<h4 id="financial-27940" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">This resolution will provide $152,364.59 for 12 months of maintenance support and managed services for the Street Smart Software application for use by the Tampa Police Department from the FY 2025 UASI Grant within the Urban Area Security Initiative Grants Fund.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Total dollars this resolution authorizes the City to spend in the current fiscal year. Inter-fund transfers are excluded to avoid double-counting."><dt>Spending authorized</dt><dd>$152,400</dd></div></dl>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$150,680.08</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Urban Area Security Initiative (UASI) Grants</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Police Administration</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Computers-Contractual Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__project">UASI 2025 - City of Tampa</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">12525.228990.534009.1002974</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">expenditure</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$859.96</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Urban Area Security Initiative (UASI) Grants</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Police Administration</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Computers-Contractual Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__project">UASI 2025 - TIA</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">12525.228990.534009.1002982</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">expenditure</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$859.96</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Urban Area Security Initiative (UASI) Grants</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Police Administration</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Computers-Contractual Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__project">UASI 2025 - USF</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">12525.228990.534009.1002983</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">expenditure</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<ol><li>The City of Tampa, Florida by authority contained in Resolution No. 2025-530 passed and adopted by City Council on Service Schedule No. 3 was passed and adopted on June 26, 2025.  The City of Tampa, Florida by authority contained in Resolution No. 2026-135 passed and adopted by City Council on Service Schedule No. 4 was passed and adopted on March 5, 2026.  It would be in the best interest of the citizens of the City of Tampa to enter into the aforementioned Service Schedule No. 5 with Versaterm Public Safety US, Inc.</li></ol><p>The City of Tampa is a current licensee of a software application now known as OpsIntel – (Formerly Street Smart) and the City desires to engage OpsIntel to provide ongoing managed services and software maintenance from July 1, 2026 through June 30, 2027.  The Software enables multiple law enforcement agencies to exchange information necessary for daily law enforcement activities by providing real time data which allows police officers to pinpoint crimes, patterns, and incidents, thus allowing for, among other things, more expeditious crime fighting.  Urban Area Working Group for UASI grant has approved purchase of OpsIntel (Formerly Street Smart).  The purchase of OpsIntel License for Tampa Police Department, University of South Florida Police Department and Tampa International Airport Police Department from the FY 2025 Urban Area Security Initiative (UASI) grant funds pursuant to Resolution No. 2026-383, passed and adopted by the City Council of the City of Tampa on May 21, 2026.  The City of Tampa, Florida by authority contained in Resolution No. 2024-976, passed and adopted by City Council on November 7, 2024, approved a Master Services Agreement and Service Schedule No. 1 with Versaterm Public Safety US.  The Master Services Agreement with Versaterm Public Safety US was amended and extended as Schedule No. 2 contained in Resolution No. 2025-529 passed and adopted on June 26,</p>
</details>

<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>

<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/27940/reso-approve-versaterm-schedule-.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">RESO APPROVE VERSATERM SCHEDULE .PDF</a></li>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-27945">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-27945">10</a> File No. PS26-27945</h3>
<p>(Part 1 of 2)(See Item 11) resolution approving the acceptance of subgrant funding in an amount up to $200,000 from the State of Florida Department of Transportation (FDOT), Highway Traffic Safety Funds, for the Tampa Police Department Safe Motorcycle and Rider Techniques (SMART) project to teach street survival skills to motorcycle riders, conduct safety focused enforcement operations, and provide education to the public through the term of the subgrant ending 9/30/27.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-27945">
<h4 id="financial-27945" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">This resolution approves the grant award of $200,000 from the State of Florida Department of Transportation (FDOT) for the Tampa Police Department&#39;s 2027 Safe Motorcycle and Rider Techniques Grant. Funds will be appropriated within the Police Grants Fund.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Outside funds (grants, fees, reimbursements) the City is committing to receive in the current fiscal year."><dt>Grant or revenue accepted</dt><dd>$200,000</dd></div></dl>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-revenue "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$200,000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Police Grants</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Police Administration</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Federal Grant - Public Safety</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__project">2027 FDOT Safe Motorcycle and Rider Techniques Grant</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">12500.228990.331200.1003041</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Revenue Increase</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>Grant funds in an amount up to $200,000 are available to the City of Tampa for use by the Tampa Police Department in the form of a subgrant through a program administered by the State of Florida Department of Transportation under FY 2027 Project Number MC-2027-00397 and Project Title Safe Motorcycle And Rider Techniques (SMART) and all future amendments with no financial modifications.  The City of Tampa desires to accept the awarded subgrant in the amount of $200,000. The grant funds, once received and deployed pursuant to the terms of the grant, will reduce motorcycle related crashes and injuries.  The Tampa Police Department has been offering the SMART program since 2020 and will have trained approximately 470 riders by end of FY 2026.  Providing motorcycle safety information and education serves a public purpose and will benefit the citizens of Tampa.</p>
</details>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28103">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28103">14</a> File No. PS26-28103</h3>
<p>(Part 1 of 2)(See Item 15) Resolution approving the acceptance of subgrant funding in an amount up to $450,000 from the State of Florida Department of Transportation (FDOT), Highway Traffic Safety Funds, for the Tampa Police Department &quot;Sit Tight and Belt Right&quot; Occupant Protection Program, to promote safety belt and child restraint use through the term of the subgrant ending 9/30/27.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-28103">
<h4 id="financial-28103" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">This resolution accepts $450,000 within the Police Grants Fund for the FY 2027 Sit Tight and Belt Right Occupant Protection grant. Funds will be deposited in the Police Grants Fund.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Outside funds (grants, fees, reimbursements) the City is committing to receive in the current fiscal year."><dt>Grant or revenue accepted (FY27)</dt><dd>$450,000</dd></div></dl>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-revenue "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$450,000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Police Grants</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Police Administration</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Federal Grant - Public Safety</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__project">2027 FDOT Sit Tight and Belt Right</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY27 · 12500.228990.331200.1003039</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Revenue Increase</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>Grant funds in an amount up to $450,000 are available to the City of Tampa for use by the Tampa Police Department in the form of a subgrant through a program administered by the State of Florida Department of Transportation under FY 2027 Project Number OP-2027-00208 and Project Title “Sit Tight and Belt Right” Occupant Protection Program.  The City of Tampa desires to accept the awarded subgrant in an amount up to $450,000.  Tampa is below average in safety belt usage and ranks near the top of large cities in the State for occupant-protection related serious injuries and fatalities.  The grant funds, once received and deployed pursuant to the terms of the grant, will help reduce traffic crash fatalities and injuries due to unrestrained or improperly restrained occupants through education and enforcement, and will provide child seats to low income families.  Entering into this agreement is determined to be in the best interest of the City of Tampa.</p>
</details>

<p>Resolution making certain changes in the budget of the City of Tampa for the fiscal year ending September 30, 2026; Approving the transfer, reallocation, and/or appropriation of $529,934 within the General Fund for use by the Tampa Police Department for the reclassification of R10 and X10 Drone lease under GASB 87 Guidelines.</p>

<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">This resolution appropriates $454,934 within the General Fund to recognize the present value of R10 and X10 drones in accordance with GASB 87 guidelines under Generally Accepted Accounting Principles for financed purchases and reappropriates $75,000 within the General Fund from operating to debt - principal leases for use by the Tampa Police Department.</p>

<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-revenue "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$454,934</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">General Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Police Dept</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Financed Purchase Proceeds Other Sources</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY26 · 01100.232400.383101</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Revenue Increase</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$454,934</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">General Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Police Dept</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Machinery and Equipment</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY26 · 01100.232400.564000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Expenditure Increase</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$75,000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">General Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Police Administration</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Principal Lease Purchase</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY26 · 01100.228990.571001</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Expenditure Increase</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure-decrease "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$75,000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">General Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Police Administration</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Repair and Maintenance Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY26 · 01100.228990.546000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Expenditure Decrease</span></li></ul>

<h3 class="agenda__committee" id="neighborhood-and-community-affairs-committee">Neighborhood and Community Affairs Committee</h3>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-27128">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-27128">17</a> File No. NCA26-27128</h3>
<p>Resolution approving the Renewal of an award between the City of Tampa and Nelson’s Tree Farm and Nursery, Inc. with a Consumer Price Index (CPI) price escalation of 3.5% for the furnishing of Mulch and-or Shell Purchased, Delivered and-or Installed (Sheltered Market Solicitation) in the estimated amount of $350,000 for use by the Parks and Recreation Department.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-27128">
<h4 id="financial-27128" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">This resolution renews the award between the City and Nelson’s Tree Farm and Nursery, Inc., with a Consumer Price Index (CPI) price escalation of 3.5%, for the furnishing of Mulch and-or Shell Purchased, Delivered and-or Installed (Sheltered Market Solicitation) in the estimated amount of $350,000, over a 12-month term, subject to annual appropriation, for use by the Parks and Recreation Department within the General Fund.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Total dollars this resolution authorizes the City to spend in the current fiscal year. Inter-fund transfers are excluded to avoid double-counting."><dt>Spending authorized (FY27)</dt><dd>$300,000</dd></div></dl>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__future">Plus <strong>$50,000</strong> in future-year amounts subject to annual Council approval.</p>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$300,000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">General Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Parks and Recreation Median Contracts</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Other Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY27 · 01100.227400.534000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Estimated Expenditure</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure is-future"><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$50,000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">General Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Parks and Recreation Median Contracts</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Other Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY28 · 01100.227400.534000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Estimated Expenditure</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__subject" title="Council is not committing this fiscal year today; subject to future-year appropriation.">subject to annual appropriation</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>It is recommended that the City renew its existing award with Nelson’s Tree Farm and Nursery, Inc. for the provision of Mulch and-or Shell Purchased, Delivered and-or Installed (SMS) for use by the Parks and Recreation Department for an additional one-year term. The existing award was made January 2024. This award is in its second renewal term.</p>
<p>Nelson’s Tree Farm and Nursery, Inc. provides excellent service, quality of product and performance. The Park and Recreation Department has reviewed the prices and feels that it is in the best interest of the City to renew the award for an additional one-year period.</p>
<p>Nelson’s Tree Farm and Nursery, Inc. has agreed to renew Mulch and-or Shell Purchased, Delivered and-or Installed (SMS) with a Consumer Price Index (CPI) price escalation of 3.5%; which is allowed per the bid.</p>
<p>Equal Business Opportunity: The awardee,  Nelson’s Tree Farm &amp; Nursery, Inc , is a certified Small Local Business Enterprise (SLBE).</p>
</details>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-27762">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-27762">18</a> File No. NCA26-27762</h3>
<p>Resolution of the City of Tampa, Florida, approving additional expenditures within an existing standardization award between the City of Tampa and Net Assets Corporation, for the furnishing of Electronic Access to Interests Against Real Property in the estimated amount of $374,539 for a cumulative total expenditure of $544,539, over a 36-month term, for use by the Neighborhood Enhancement Department.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-27762">
<h4 id="financial-27762" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">That this Resolution shall provide an additional $374,539, subject to annual appropriations, for a cumulative amount of $544,539 for use by the Neighborhood Enhancement Department within the General Fund.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Total dollars this resolution authorizes the City to spend in the current fiscal year. Inter-fund transfers are excluded to avoid double-counting."><dt>Spending authorized (FY27)</dt><dd>$123,003</dd></div></dl>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__future">Plus <strong>$251,536</strong> in future-year amounts subject to annual Council approval.</p>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$123,002.67</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">General Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Neighborhood Enhancement</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Other-Contractual Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY27 · 01100.220700.534007</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Estimated Expenditure</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure is-future"><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$188,191.33</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">General Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Neighborhood Enhancement</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Other-Contractual Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY28 · 01100.220700.534007</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Estimated Expenditure, subject to annual appropriation</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__subject" title="Council is not committing this fiscal year today; subject to future-year appropriation.">subject to annual appropriation</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure is-future"><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$63,345</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">General Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Neighborhood Enhancement</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Other-Contractual Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY29 · 01100.220700.534007</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Estimated Expenditure, subject to annual appropriation</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__subject" title="Council is not committing this fiscal year today; subject to future-year appropriation.">subject to annual appropriation</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>The Neighborhood Enhancement Department has standardized to Electronic Access to Interests Against Real Property through web-based service Conduits from Net Assets Corporation. Standardization to this service ensures uninterrupted delivery of service as well as reduction of redundancy and costly expenditures for implementation of a new service.</p>
<p>It is recommended that the City purchase Electronic Access to Interests Against Real Property through web-based service Conduits. Being this is a service transaction, a memo was included with an estimated list of services planned to use per year based on previous usage.</p>
<p>Equal Business Opportunity (EBO) Statement: There was no EBO participation, as this purchase is being made under a standardization.</p>
</details>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-27793">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-27793">19</a> File No. NCA26-27793</h3>
<p>Resolution assessing the costs expended by the City of Tampa, a municipal corporation, for the preparation, demolition and/or removal of certain buildings and/or structures from the following properties: (1) 3517 E 27th Ave, and (2) 4606 N 29th St, after the determination that said building or structure satisfied the criteria to demolish and the property owner(s) failed to demolish and/or remove same or failed to do so prior to the City incurring costs; authorizing a certified copy of the resolution to be recorded with the Clerk of the Circuit Court and having the force and effect of a notice of assessment of lien.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-27793">
<h4 id="financial-27793" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">To impose lien in an effort to recover the required administrative fee and hard costs expended by the City. Revenue will be received by the Neighborhood Enhancement Division within the General Fund.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Outside funds (grants, fees, reimbursements) the City is committing to receive in the current fiscal year."><dt>Grant or revenue accepted</dt><dd>$20,619</dd></div></dl>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-revenue "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$20,618.93</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">General Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Neighborhood Enhancement</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Structure Liens-Hard Cost</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">01100.220700.354003</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Revenue</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>This resolution assesses the costs ($20,618.93) expended by the City of Tampa, including administrative costs, for the preparation, demolition and/or removal of buildings and/or structures on the properties described in Exhibit “A” of the Resolution that satisfied the criteria to demolish set forth in Section 19-5(a)(2) &amp; (b)(1), City of Tampa Code of Ordinances when the property owner(s) failed to demolish and/or remove the unsafe building and/or structure within the time allowed or failed to do so prior to the City incurring said costs.</p>
</details>

<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-27794">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-27794">20</a> File No. NCA26-27794</h3>
<p>Resolution approving the First Amendment to the Agreement between the City of Tampa and Momentus Technologies, LLC to provide Software for a Platform for Maintaining Special Event Administrative Operations in the amount of forty-eight thousand seven hundred and twenty dollars ($48,720.00); authorizing execution by the Mayor and attestation by the City Clerk; providing an effective date nunc pro tunc to July 1, 2026.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-27794">
<h4 id="financial-27794" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">This resolution approves the agreement between the City of Tampa and Momentus Technologies, LLC, attached hereto and made a part hereof, to provide software for a platform for maintaining special event administrative operations additional services in the amount of Forty-Eight Thousand Seven Hundred and Twenty Dollars ($48,720.00) for use by the Parks and Recreation department within the General Fund.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Total dollars this resolution authorizes the City to spend in the current fiscal year. Inter-fund transfers are excluded to avoid double-counting."><dt>Spending authorized (FY27)</dt><dd>$48,720</dd></div></dl>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$48,720.00</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">General Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Parks and Recreation Budget</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Computers -  Bulk Purchases Hardware/Software</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY27 · 01100.225700.552007</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">expenditure</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>Pursuant to the authority contained in Resolution No. 2024-594, passed and adopted by the City Council on July 18, 2024, the City of Tampa and Momentus Technologies, LLC approved an order form agreement to provide Software for a Platform for Maintaining Special Event Administrative Operations for use by the Parks and Recreation Department Office of Special Events.</p>
<p>It is necessary to amend the agreement to include invoicing, application, calendaring, and permitting event services in the software platform being utilized by Parks and Recreation Office of Special Events.</p>
<p>Equal Business Opportunity (EBO) Statement: There was no EBO participation, as this purchase is being made under a sole-source master subscription agreement.</p>
</details>

<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-27948">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-27948">21</a> File No. NCA26-27948</h3>
<p>Resolution of the City of Tampa, Florida accepting a donation of a bat house from Amaryllis Garden Circle that is valued at one hundred fifty dollars ($150) to support the City of Tampa Parks &amp; Recreation Department; authorizing the Mayor of the City of Tampa to execute the acceptance of said donation.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>The City of Tampa desires to accept a donation of a Bat House from Amaryllis Garden Circle (“Donor”) at an estimated value of One Hundred Fifty Dollars ($150). Acceptance of the donation is being undertaken in a manner consistent with the City Acceptance of Gifts &amp; Donation Policy.  Donor has agreed to execute the Instrument of Donation confirming its intention to donate one Bat House valued at One Hundred Fifty Dollars ($150) to support the City of Tampa Parks and Recreation Department (a copy of which is attached hereto as Exhibit A).  City Council does hereby affirm that it would be beneficial to the residents of the City to approve acceptance of the donation.</p>
</details>

<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/27948/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/27948/resolution-and-instrument-of-donation.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">RESOLUTION AND INSTRUMENT OF DONATION.PDF</a></li>
</ul>
</div>
</article>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28107">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28107">22</a> File No. NCA26-28107</h3>
<p>Resolution requiring the Parks &amp; Recreation Department to notify City Council prior to removal or replacement of the Equal Justice Initiative Historical Marker sited on the City of Tampa Riverwalk at Doyl Carlton Drive and West Laurel Street.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>In 2022 through the Tampa- Hillsborough Community Remembrance Project Equal Justice Initiative, a historical marker of the incident specific to Robert Johnson was erected on the Tampa Riverwalk.  This historical marker stands as a memorial on the City of Tampa Riverwalk to honor the memory of Mr. Johnson and the four other African American men lynched during the Jim Crow era.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/28107/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/28107/resolution-robert-johnson-historic-marker.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">RESOLUTION - ROBERT JOHNSON HISTORIC MARKER.PDF</a></li>
</ul>
</div>
</article>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28140">

<p>Resolution making certain changes in the budget of the City of Tampa for the fiscal year ending September 30, 2027; approving the transfer, reallocation, and/or appropriation of $9,831.25 within the General Fund.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">

<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Total dollars this resolution authorizes the City to spend in the current fiscal year. Inter-fund transfers are excluded to avoid double-counting."><dt>Spending authorized</dt><dd>$9,831</dd></div></dl>

</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>The Parks and Recreation Department is in a Lease agreement with the Tampa JCC federation. Funds are being transferred into debt expenditure accounts in order to pay the monthly lease payment.</p>

Operations</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Golf Courses Operations and CIP</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Operating Expense Budget Reserve</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">41800.222205.539999</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Expenditure Increase</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-revenue "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$12,796</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Golf Courses - Capital/Construction</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Golf Courses Operations and CIP</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Improv Other Than Bldgs</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__project">Rogers Park Golf Course</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">41900.222205.563001.1000969</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Expenditure Decrease</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure-decrease "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$142,662</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Golf Courses - Capital/Construction</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Golf Courses Operations and CIP</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Improv Other Than Bldgs</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__project">Rocky Point Golf Course</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">41900.222205.563001.1000970</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Expenditure Decrease</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure-decrease "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$154,987</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Golf Courses - Capital/Construction</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Golf Courses Operations and CIP</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Improv Other Than Bldgs</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__project">Babe Zaharias Golf Course</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">41900.222205.563001.1000971</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Expenditure Decrease</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>The City is required to report the financial operations of its three golf courses, which are managed and operated by the Tampa Sports Authority (TSA). This Resolution provides the necessary revenue and expenditure appropriations to reflect these golf course operations in the City’s annual financial report, as reported by the TSA.</p>
<p>Additionally, capital expenditures for the City’s golf courses are budgeted in the Golf Courses Capital/Construction Fund. Under the City of Tampa’s Capital Assets and Depreciation Policy, any fixed asset costing less than $5,000 or with a useful life of fewer than two years must be classified as an operating expense. This Resolution realigns FY2026 funding from the Golf Courses Capital/Construction Fund to the Golf Courses Operations Fund to ensure that the expenditure budget aligns with the appropriate cost categories in accordance with City policy.</p>
<p>Pursuant to Section 166.241(8)(a), under certain circumstances a local government within 60 days following the end of the fiscal year may amend a budget for that year.</p>
</details>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28234">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28234">26</a> File No. NCA26-28234</h3>
<p>Resolution approving a services agreement for persons with intellectual disabilities internship administrator services between the city of Tampa, Florida and Pepin Academies, Inc. A Florida not for profit corporation to support the Mike Phillips All Abilities Internship Program which was designed to connect Neuro-Diverse Learners to internship opportunities with the City of Tampa in an amount not to exceed fifteen thousand dollars ($15,000).</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-28234">
<h4 id="financial-28234" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">This Resolution approves an agreement between the city of Tampa and Pepin Academies, Inc. for an amount not to exceed $15,000 to promote the educational and transitional needs of students with identified intellectual disabilities through internship opportunities with the City for use by the Neighborhood and Community Affairs Department within the General Fund.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Total dollars this resolution authorizes the City to spend in the current fiscal year. Inter-fund transfers are excluded to avoid double-counting."><dt>Spending authorized</dt><dd>$15,000</dd></div></dl>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$15,000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">General Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Neighborhood Workforce Partnerships &amp; Special Projects</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Internship Program for Persons with Intellectual Disabilities Contribution</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">01100.220320.582546</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Expenditure</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>It is in the public interest of the City of Tampa (&quot;City&#39;0, to provide services through Pepin Academies Inc. (&#39;Agency&#39;) for residents of the City who are desirous of such services; and pursuant to this Services Agreement for Persons with Intellectual Disabilities Internship Administrator Services (&#39;Agreement&#39;) the Agency shall provide greater opportunities for neuro-diverse learners to transition more effectively into the workforce by providing internship opportunities in selected City departments; and it is the desire of the parties to establish the terms and conditions under which the terms of the Agreement shall be implemented; and the term of this Agreement is for one year with no guaranteed renewal for subsequent terms; and the City shall reimburse the Agency in an amount not to exceed Fifteen Thousand Dollars ($15,000) for referring eligible persons to intern with the City in different capacities as selected  and determined by the City; and City Council does hereby affirm that it would be beneficial to the residents of the City to enter into this Agreement between the City and the Agency.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/28234/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/28234/pepin-academies-fy27-agreement-and-reso.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">PEPIN ACADEMIES FY27 AGREEMENT AND RESO.PDF</a></li>
</ul>
</div>
</article>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-27734">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-27734">34</a> File No. INF26-27734</h3>
<p>Resolution approving the bid of Continental Carbon Group USA Inc. for the furnishing of Granular Activated Carbon (GAC) and Sand Filter Media (Rebid) in the estimated amount of $900,000 for use by the Water Department.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-27734">
<h4 id="financial-27734" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">This Resolution approves an award between the City and Continental Carbon Group USA Inc. for the furnishing of Granular Activated Carbon (GAC) and Sand Filter Media (Rebid) and provides an estimated amount of $900,000 for use by the Water Department within the Water Operations Fund.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Total dollars this resolution authorizes the City to spend in the current fiscal year. Inter-fund transfers are excluded to avoid double-counting."><dt>Spending authorized (FY27)</dt><dd>$900,000</dd></div></dl>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$900,000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Water - Operations</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Water Production Treatment</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Chemicals - Outside Vendors</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY27 · 40400.246100.552002</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Estimated Expenditure</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<ol><li>Department or Location of Project: Water Department, Production Division</li><li>Total number of vendors that accessed and downloaded bid: 29</li><li>Number and List of bidders: five, Carbon Activated Corp, Continental Carbon Group USA Inc, CribProcure Inc., Fehl Safe Water Systems, Norit Americas Inc.</li><li>Award amount: $900,000.00</li><li>Why the winning bid was selected: The City of Tampa Water Department, Production Division recommends awarding this to Continental Carbon Group USA Inc as the lowest responsive and responsible bidder for Granular Activated Carbon (GAC) and Sand Filter Media (Rebid).</li></ol><p>It is recommended that the City award Granular Activated Carbon (GAC) and Sand Filter Media (Rebid) to Continental Carbon Group USA Inc for use by the Water Department, Production Division.  The term of the award is for a (1) one year term with (3) three additional (1) one year renewal options. The Department has reviewed and approved the bid response from Continental Carbon Group USA Inc and comments were supplied via Department recommendation letter.</p>
<p>Equal Business Opportunity (EBO) Statement: Pursuant to the review by the Equal Business Opportunity (EBO) Division, it was determined that SLBE participation goals were not prescribed in the solicitation because 2 or more firms were not ready, willing, and able.</p>
</details>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-27736">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-27736">36</a> File No. INF26-27736</h3>
<p>Resolution of the City of Tampa, Florida approving a standardization agreement between the City of Tampa and Getinge USA Sales, LLC for the provision of Getinge Autoclave Parts and Service for the Getinge Autoclave, in the estimated amount of $25,939.97, over a 36-month term, for use by the Wastewater Department.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-27736">
<h4 id="financial-27736" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">That this Resolution approves said Agreement between the City of Tampa and Getinge USA Sales, LLC for Getinge Autoclave Parts and Service in an estimated amount of $25,939.97 over a 36-month term for use by the Wastewater Department within the Wastewater Operations Fund.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Total dollars this resolution authorizes the City to spend in the current fiscal year. Inter-fund transfers are excluded to avoid double-counting."><dt>Spending authorized (FY27)</dt><dd>$8,647</dd></div></dl>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__future">Plus <strong>$17,293</strong> in future-year amounts subject to annual Council approval.</p>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$8,646.65</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Wastewater - Operations</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Wastewater Labs</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Repair and Maintenance Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY27 · 41000.243700.546000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Estimated Expenditure</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure is-future"><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$8,646.67</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Wastewater - Operations</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Wastewater Labs</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Repair and Maintenance Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY28 · 41000.243700.546000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Estimated Expenditure</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__subject" title="Council is not committing this fiscal year today; subject to future-year appropriation.">subject to annual appropriation</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure is-future"><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$8,646.65</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Wastewater - Operations</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Wastewater Labs</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Repair and Maintenance Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY29 · 41000.243700.546000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Estimated Expenditure</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__subject" title="Council is not committing this fiscal year today; subject to future-year appropriation.">subject to annual appropriation</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>The Wastewater Department has standardized to Getinge Autoclave Parts and Service from Getinge USA Sales, LLC.  Standardization to this equipment and service ensures cost reduction, interoperability because of familiarity among staff, maintenance efficiency and reduced cost of inventory maintenance for parts as well as uninterrupted delivery of service, reduction of redundancy and costly expenditures for implementation of a new service.  It is recommended that the City purchase Getinge Autoclave Parts and Service.</p>
<p>Equal Business Opportunity (EBO) Statement: There was no EBO Participation as this purchase is being made under a standardization.</p>
</details>

<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-27737">37</a> File No. INF26-27737</h3>
<p>Resolution approving the renewal of an award between the City of Tampa and Southeast Pump Specialist, Inc., without a price escalation, for the furnishing of Pump Repairs for the Water Department in the estimated amount of $500,000, over a 24-month term, for use by the Water Department.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-27737">
<h4 id="financial-27737" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">This Resolution renews the award between the City and Southeast Pump Specialist, Inc. for the furnishing of Pump Repairs for the Water Department in the estimated amount of $500,000, over a 24-month term, subject to annual appropriation, for use by the Water Department within the Water Operations Fund.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Total dollars this resolution authorizes the City to spend in the current fiscal year. Inter-fund transfers are excluded to avoid double-counting."><dt>Spending authorized (FY27)</dt><dd>$228,800</dd></div></dl>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__future">Plus <strong>$271,200</strong> in future-year amounts subject to annual Council approval.</p>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$228,800</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Water - Operations</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Water Production Maintenance</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Other-Contractual Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY27 · 40400.246000.534007</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Estimated Expenditure</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure is-future"><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$250,000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Water - Operations</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Water Production Maintenance</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Other-Contractual Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY28 · 40400.246000.534007</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Estimated Expenditure</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__subject" title="Council is not committing this fiscal year today; subject to future-year appropriation.">subject to annual appropriation</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure is-future"><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$21,200</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Water - Operations</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Water Production Maintenance</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Other-Contractual Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY29 · 40400.246000.534007</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Estimated Expenditure</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__subject" title="Council is not committing this fiscal year today; subject to future-year appropriation.">subject to annual appropriation</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>It is recommended that the City renew its existing award with Southeast Pump Specialists, Inc. for the provision of Pump Repairs for the Water Department for an additional two-year period.  The existing award became effective October 17, 2024. This award is in its initial contract period.</p>
<p>Southeast Pump Specialists, Inc. provide satisfactory service, quality of product and performance. The Water Department has reviewed the prices and feels that it is in the best interest of the City to renew the award for an additional two-year period.  Southeast Pump Specialists, Inc. has agreed to renew the contract for Pump Repairs for the Water Department.</p>
<p>Equal Business Opportunity (EBO) Statement: Pursuant to the review by the Equal Business Opportunity (EBO) Division, this two-year renewal contract is in compliance.</p>
</details>

Dollar amount: • The Original Contract Amount was $298,940.00, • Change Order 1 Final is $0.00, therefore the Contract Amount remains unchanged.</li><li>Contractor/Firm:  J &amp; M Materials of Riverview, Inc.</li><li>Business Case: a. This project provided for the construction of an asphalt parking lot with 39 parking spaces including site preparation, grading, compaction; installation of stormwater facilities including detention ponds, structures and pipes; asphalt paving, striping; installation of concrete curbs, tree removal; installation of light poles and associated electrical work.</li><li>Equal Business Opportunity (EBO) Statement: J &amp; M Materials of Riverview, Inc., the prime contractor,  is a certified Small Local Business Enterprise (SLBE) and has utilized one (1) City of Tampa-certified firm for subcontracting.</li><li>Sustainability and Resiliency: N/A</li><li>Community Engagement: N/A</li><li>Project: a. <article class="agenda-item" id="item-27941">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-27941">39</a> File No. INF26-27941</h3>
<p>Resolution accepting the proposal of Green Loop Construction, LLC pertaining to Contract 26-C-00031 Stormwater Pipe Point Repair and Replacement Contract FY26, In the amount of $1,167,400.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-27941">
<h4 id="financial-27941" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">This resolution accepts the proposal between the City of Tampa and Green Loop Construction, LLC for the Stormwater Pipe Point Repair and Replacement Contract in the amount of $1,167,400 for use by the Mobility Department within the Stormwater Service Assessment Fund.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Total dollars this resolution authorizes the City to spend in the current fiscal year. Inter-fund transfers are excluded to avoid double-counting."><dt>Spending authorized (FY27)</dt><dd>$1,167,400</dd></div></dl>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$1,167,400.00</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Stormwater Service Assessment Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">TSS - Stormwater Operations</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Repair and Maintenance Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY27 · 10700.234300.546000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Expenditure</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<ol><li>Background: a. Contract Title: Stormwater Pipe Point Repair and Replacement Contract FY26 b. Address of Project: Various locations throughout the City c. Number of bidders/submitters: Seven (7) d.    Names of bidders/submitters: • Green Loop Construction, LLC • Fehl Safe Water Systems LLC • Southern Road &amp; Bridge, LLC • Harris-McBurney Company • Stage Door II, LLC • Rowland (DE) LLC • Carl Hankins, Inc.</li><li>Dollar amount: $1,167,400.00</li><li>Why the awardee was selected: Contractor was selected via hard bid</li><li>Contractor/Firm: Green Loop Construction, LLC</li><li>Business Case:  Project provides for repairing stormwater pipe cave-ins with all associated work required for a complete project in accordance with the contract documents This is a one (1) year contract that can be renewed for three (3) additional one (1) year period at the same prices, terms and Conditions. Work is issued by location on a work order basis.</li><li>Equal Business Opportunity (EBO) Statement:  Green Loop Construction, LLC, the prime contractor, is a certified Small Local Business Enterprise (SLBE) and is committed to utilizing four (4) city-certified firms for subcontracting.</li><li>Sustainability and Resiliency: The project provides for stormwater pipe improvements at various locations throughout the City. This project is consistent with Transforming Tampa&#39;s Tomorrow goal of Sustainability and Resiliency and is integral to protect properties and infrastructure from flood damage.</li><li>Project: a. Contract delivery method: This is a Design-Bid- Build project. A full-time City of Tampa Construction Inspector will be on-site during the project to ensure quality of construction.</li></ol><p>Community Engagement: The Mobility Public Relations and CAD Construction, Engineering and Inspection (CAD-CEI) team will assist the Contractor in the communication efforts to ensure transparency and integration of the stakeholders.</p>
<p>b. Placemaking: N/A c. Time, Quality Assurance, Cost Monitoring (Budget) •  365 consecutive calendar days.</p>

<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Total dollars this resolution authorizes the City to spend in the current fiscal year. Inter-fund transfers are excluded to avoid double-counting."><dt>Spending authorized</dt><dd>$1,662,233</dd></div></dl>

<ol><li>Contract Title: 23-C-00004, Yorkshire Watermain Replacement a. Address of Project: Yorkshire Neighborhood b. Number of bidders/submitters: N/A c. Names of bidders/submitters: N/A d. Dollar amount: $781,022.21 e. Why the awardee was selected: Project was competitively bid with the selection of the apparent low bidder.</li><li>Contractor/Firm: Dallas 1 Corporation &amp; Development</li><li>Business Case: Project provided for the furnishing and installation of approximately 10,628 linear feet of 4-inch diameter and 17,127 linear feet of 6 and 8 -inch diameter ductile iron water main with all required appurtenances and fittings including cutting and plugging, roadway and roadside restoration, traffic control, tree protection, grouting of abandoned pipe, valve adjustment and removal, video photography.</li><li>Equal Business Opportunity (EBO) Statement:  Dallas 1 Corporation dba Dallas 1 Construction &amp; Development, the prime contractor, has utilized three (3) City of Tampa-certified firms for subcontracting.</li><li>Sustainability and Resiliency: This project is consistent with &quot;Transforming Tampa&#39;s Tomorrow&quot; Goal of Sustainability and Resiliency and is part of the Water Department&#39;s PIPES Program focused on the replacement of aging infrastructure. Approximately five (5.25) miles of water mains will be replaced within the Yorkshire neighborhood area as part of this project.</li><li>Community Engagement: The CAD Construction, Engineering and Inspection (CAD-CEI) team will assist the Contractor in the communication efforts to ensure transparency and integration of the stakeholders.</li><li>Project: a. Contract delivery method: This is a Bid project. A full-time City of Tampa Construction Inspector will be on-site during the project to ensure quality of construction.</li></ol><p>Background:</p>
<p>This Change Order 1 is the final change order to provide for the release of retainage, overruns, as-built plans, installation of casing, speed humps, meter relocations, maintenance of traffic and mobilizations, and the extension of the contract for 306 additional calendar days.</p>

</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<ol><li>Contractor/Firm: Himes Electric Company, Inc</li><li>Business Case: This lighting project (Project), at multiple city athletic fields, maintains and improves the quality and longevity of the current lighting system in operation. The City of Tampa entered into an agreement with Himes Electric Company, Inc. on September 19, 2024 to provide certain professional design-build services under Contract 23-C-00014 for the Project.</li><li>Equal Business Opportunity (EBO) Statement: Himes Electric Company, Inc., the prime contractor, has utilized one (1) City of Tampa-certified firm for subcontracting.</li><li>Sustainability and Resiliency: All new construction for this project shall make the best use of all applicable and sustainable materials and methodologies to reduce operating costs.</li><li>Community Engagement: The CAD Construction, Engineering and Inspection (CAD-CEI) team assisted the Contractor in the communication efforts to ensure transparency and integration of the stakeholders.</li><li>Project: a. A full-time City of Tampa Construction Inspector was on-site during the project to ensure quality of construction.</li></ol><p>1.Contract Title: 23-C-00014 Athletic Field Lighting Design Build GMP-01 a. Dollar amount: $202,772.69 Decrease, $5,000 final payment b. Why the awardee was selected: Selected Pursuant to FS 287.055 CCNA.</p>
<p>GMP-1 Agreement approved on August 28, 2025, provided construction services to maintain and improve the quality and longevity of the current lighting system at Rowlett Park, Calvin Taylor Park, and Himes Avenue Sports Complex with a Guaranteed Maximum Price in the amount of $1,955,492; GMP 2 Agreement approved on May 21, 2026 provided construction services for at American Legion Park, Gadsden Park, and Perio &amp; Albany Park with a Guaranteed Maximum Price in the amount of $2,253,509.50; Change Order Final to GMP 1 provides for a decrease in the amount of $202,772.69 to GMP 1 revising contracted amount from 1,955,492 to  $1,752,719.31  and authorizes final payment for GMP 1 in the amount of $5,000.00 and establishes a new completion date of August 17, 2026;</p>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28132">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28132">43</a> File No. INF26-28132</h3>
<p>(Part 1 of 2)(See Item 44) Resolution of the City of Tampa, Florida, approving a Standard Grant Agreement (No. L0556) between the City of Tampa and the State of Florida Department of Environmental Protection for acceptance of grant funds in an amount not to exceed $1,000,000 to support the Tampa Ditch Rehabilitation Program; Authorizing execution thereof by the Mayor of the City of Tampa and attestation by the City Clerk; Providing an effective date of July 1, 2026, nunc pro tunc.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-28132">
<h4 id="financial-28132" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">The resolution accepts a grant in the amount of $1,000,000 from the State of Florida Department of Environmental Protection to provide funding for the Tampa Ditch Rehabilitation Program for use by the Mobility Department within the Stormwater Grants Fund.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Outside funds (grants, fees, reimbursements) the City is committing to receive in the current fiscal year."><dt>Grant or revenue accepted</dt><dd>$1,000,000</dd></div></dl>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-revenue "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$1,000,000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Stormwater Grants Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Stormwater Capital Project Costs</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Fl Dept of Environmental Protection Rev</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__project">Ditch Rehabilitation</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">30900.241800.334302.1000751</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Revenue</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>The City of Tampa applied for and was awarded a state appropriation to support the Tampa Ditch Rehabilitation Program in the amount of $1,000,000. There is no required match for this Standard Grant Agreement. Grant funds will be used to rehabilitate the Lamb Canal by stabilizing eroded banks and improving conveyance within the canal and its connecting ditches.</p>
</details>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28133">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28133">45</a> File No. INF26-28133</h3>
<p>Resolution authorizing Change Order 3 Final to Contract 23-C-00003 Cold Milling of Existing Asphaltic Concrete Roadways, between the City of Tampa and Turtle Infrastructure Partners, LLC, an increase and final payment of $124,935.49; authorizing execution of said Change Order by the Mayor of the City of Tampa.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-28133">
<h4 id="financial-28133" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">This Resolution provides funding for Change Order 3 Final, an increase to the existing contract and final payment in the amount of $124,935.49 for cold milling of existing asphaltic concrete roadways for use by the Mobility Department within the Local Option Gas Tax Capital Projects Fund.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Total dollars this resolution authorizes the City to spend in the current fiscal year. Inter-fund transfers are excluded to avoid double-counting."><dt>Spending authorized</dt><dd>$124,935</dd></div></dl>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$124,935.49</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Local Option Gas Tax Capital Projects</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Transportation Division Capital Project Costs</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Improv Other Than Bldgs</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__project">Street Resurfacing</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">30600.243000.563001.1002350</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Expenditure</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<ol><li>Background: a. Contract Title: 23-C-00003, Cold Milling of Existing Asphaltic Concrete Roadways b. Address of Project: Various locations throughout the City c. Dollar amount:   $124,935.49 d. Why the awardee was selected: Bidder was the lowest responsive and responsible bidders meeting all conditions of the bid The basis of award referred to in Item I-2.11 of Instructions to Bidders shall be the greatest amount of work, which can be accomplished within the funds available as budgeted.</li><li>Business Case: Project provided for cold milling of existing roadways within the City of Tampa as part of the Pavement Management Program. This was a one-year annual contract which was renewed for 2 additional 1 year periods.</li><li>Equal Business Opportunity (EBO) Statement: Turtle Infrastructure Partners, LLC, the prime contractor, has utilized one (1) City of Tampa-certified firm for subcontracting.</li><li>Sustainability and Resiliency: The project provided for roadway improvements at various locations throughout the City. This project is consistent with Transforming Tampa&#39;s Tomorrow goal of Sustainability and Resiliency and is an integral part of the Pavement Management Program.</li><li>Community Engagement: The Mobility Public Relations staff assisted the Contractor in the communication efforts to ensure transparency and integration of the stakeholders.</li><li>Project: a. Contract delivery method: This is a Design-Bid- Build project. A full-time City of Tampa Construction Inspector will be on-site during the project to ensure quality of construction.</li></ol><p>2.Contractor/Firm: Turtle Infrastructure Partners, LLC</p>
<p>Change Order 1 provided for continual services; the contract completion date was changed.</p>
<p>Change Order 2 provided for continual services; the contract completion date was changed.</p>
<p>Change Order 3, Final provides for adjustment of the Contract amount (increase), establishment of the Contract completion date, and with Final Payment to the Contractor.</p>
<p>b. Placemaking: N/A c. Time, Quality Assurance, Cost Monitoring (Budget) •  0 consecutive calendar days.</p>
<p>•  The e-Builder project management software system was utilized to optimize the monitoring of schedules and costs. This software was recently purchased by the City of Tampa and is a premier system for construction project management.</p>
</details>
