<section class="agenda__section">
<h2 id="ordinances-presented-for-first-reading-consideration-items-95-through-109-set-for-time-certain-of-1-30-p-m-per-the-request-of-the-chair">ORDINANCES PRESENTED FOR FIRST READING CONSIDERATION (Items 95 through 109)(Set for time certain of 1:30 p.m. per the request of the Chair)</h2>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28297">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28297">95</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 2 - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida, submitting to the electors of the City a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to create Section 2.15; providing the ability to City Council to override and thus render void an executive order promulgated by the Mayor under the charter.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>It creates Section 2.15 of the Tampa Charter giving City Council the authority, by an affirmative vote of at least five members acting by resolution, to override and void a mayoral executive order issued under the Charter, and it bars the Mayor from reissuing that order without Council approval.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/28297/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/28297/proposed-charter-amendment-change-final-ordinance-for-section-2.15.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">PROPOSED CHARTER AMENDMENT CHANGE - FINAL ORDINANCE FOR SECTION 2.15.PDF</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/28297/bie-proposed-charter-amendment-change-final-ordinance-for-section-2.15.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">BIE - PROPOSED CHARTER AMENDMENT CHANGE - FINAL ORDINANCE FOR SECTION 2.15.PDF</a></li>
</ul>
</div>
</article>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28295">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28295">96</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 2 - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida, submitting to the electors of the city a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 2.04 to clarify that City Council may create and fill additional staff positions to assist City Council as long as the funds are allocated within City Council’s budget; removing the requirement for prior mayoral approval.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>This amendment to Section 2.04 of the Tampa Charter would clarify that City Council may create and fill additional staff positions for any Council function and remove the requirement for prior mayoral approval, provided funding is available within the Council’s budget.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/28295/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/28295/proposed-charter-amendment-change-final-ordinance-for-section-2.04.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">PROPOSED CHARTER AMENDMENT CHANGE - FINAL ORDINANCE FOR SECTION 2.04.PDF</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/28295/bie-proposed-charter-amendment-change-final-ordinance-for-section-2.04.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">BIE - PROPOSED CHARTER AMENDMENT CHANGE - FINAL ORDINANCE FOR SECTION 2.04.PDF</a></li>
</ul>
</div>
<p class="agenda-item__notice agenda-item__notice--updated">📋 <strong>Updated by Addendum</strong> — see <a href="#addendum">Addendum</a> for details</p>
</article>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28294">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28294">97</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 2 - An Ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the City a proposed amendment to the revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 2.03 clarifying City Council’s ability to require their member’s attendance.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>This amendment to Section 2.03 of the Tampa Charter would clarify City Council’s authority to adopt its own bylaws, regulations, and procedural rules, and would change the requirement for member attendance at meetings from “compel” to “require.”</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/28294/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/28294/proposed-charter-amendment-change-final-ordinance-for-section-2.03.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">PROPOSED CHARTER AMENDMENT CHANGE - FINAL ORDINANCE FOR SECTION 2.03.PDF</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/28294/bie-proposed-charter-amendment-change-final-ordinance-for-section-2.03.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">BIE - PROPOSED CHARTER AMENDMENT CHANGE - FINAL ORDINANCE FOR SECTION 2.03.PDF</a></li>
</ul>
</div>
</article>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28296">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28296">98</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 2 - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the city a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 2.08 clarifying that the ordinance reading procedures must follow general law and clarifying that three City Council members must vote in the affirmative to require the whole text of a proposed ordinance be read at final reading, as opposed to only the title of the ordinance, when general law requires two readings of the ordinance.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>It clarifies in Section 2.08 of the Tampa Charter that general law governs ordinance-reading procedures and that three City Council members must vote to require a full reading of the ordinance text on final reading instead of only the title.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/28296/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/28296/proposed-charter-amendment-change-final-ordinance-for-section-2.08.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">PROPOSED CHARTER AMENDMENT CHANGE - FINAL ORDINANCE FOR SECTION 2.08.PDF</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/28296/bie-proposed-charter-amendment-change-final-ordinance-for-section-2.08.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">BIE - PROPOSED CHARTER AMENDMENT CHANGE - FINAL ORDINANCE FOR SECTION 2.08.PDF</a></li>
</ul>
</div>
<p class="agenda-item__notice agenda-item__notice--updated">📋 <strong>Updated by Addendum</strong> — see <a href="#addendum">Addendum</a> for details</p>
</article>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28298">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28298">99</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 4 - An Ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the City a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 4.01 and create Section 4.03 to strike the current mayoral term limit provision and replace it with a more restrictive provision that mandates no person who has served as mayor for more than six years may be elected as mayor again; providing that such provision applies only to time served as mayor after adoption of said amendment.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>This amendment would revise Section 4.01 and add Section 4.03 of the Tampa Charter to replace the current mayoral term-limit provision with a stricter rule. Under the change, anyone who has served more than six years as mayor after the amendment takes effect would no longer be eligible to be elected as mayor again.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/28298/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28299">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28299">100</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 6 - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the City a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 6.02 clarifying the four-year term of office under this section applies only to the mayor and City Council members and removing language referencing 1979 adoption.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>It clarifies that Section 6.02’s four-year term applies only to the mayor and City Council members and removes outdated language referencing the 1979 municipal election.</p>
</details>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28302">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28302">101</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 6 - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the City a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 6.06 to remove obsolete language; clarify that a majority vote under said section requires no fewer than four members of City Council to increase or decrease salaries of officers of the City; provide that City Council may act under said section pursuant to resolution or ordinance.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>It clarifies that Section 6.06 requires at least four City Council members to vote in favor of any change to city officers’ salaries and specifies that Council may take such action through either a resolution or an ordinance.</p>
</details>

<p>CH 7 - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the city a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 7.05 to ensure accordance with state law.</p>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28304">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28304">104</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 7 - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the City a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 7.07 to allow City Council to initiate midyear budget changes without the need for mayoral recommendation and in a manner consistent with general law.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>If approved, this amendment to section 7.07 of the Tampa Charter would remove the requirement for a Mayor’s recommendation before City Council reallocates unused budgeted funds or appropriates unappropriated cash surplus for any lawful municipal purpose. .</p>
</details>

<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28305">105</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 9 - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the city a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 9.02 to remove the obsolete requirement that the chief building inspector shall be the ex officio director of the Zoning Adjustment Board.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>It amends Section 9.02 of the Tampa Charter to remove the obsolete provision stating that the chief building official serves as an ex officio director on the zoning adjustment board, eliminating outdated language.</p>
</details>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28307">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28307">106</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 10 - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the City a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 10.07 to provide a procedure for citizen’s initiatives and referendums that is in accordance with general law.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>This measure would amend Section 10.07 of the Tampa Charter to set citizen-initiated and referendum procedures by ordinance consistent with state law, allow changes to those procedures only every two years and only 90 days after a general election, and prohibit initiatives or referendums on zoning and comprehensive plan amendments as required by statute.</p>
</details>

<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>

</ul>
</div>
</article>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28309">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28309">109</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 12 - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the City a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to create Article XII, Section 12.01 creating the appointed position of and an Office of Inspector General; setting the office’s purpose; providing for independent activity and non-interference by City officials; authorizing use of legal and human resources city services; requiring an implementation ordinance be adopted on a certain schedule; specifying the role of the office under the charter; granting certain powers including subpoena authority; requiring cooperation by city officers, employees, and contractors; setting appointment method, term, removal method, and process to fill vacancies by City Council; providing for office budget request process.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>would add Article XII, Section 12.01 to the Tampa Charter to establish an Inspector General who operates independently from the City’s executive and legislative branches, empowered to conduct investigations, audits, and reviews. The amendment requires the City Council to adopt an implementing ordinance defining the office’s authority and procedures, and it places the Inspector General’s appointment, term, and removal under City Council oversight.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/28309/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/28309/proposed-charter-amendment-change-final-ordinance-for-section-12.01.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">PROPOSED CHARTER AMENDMENT CHANGE - FINAL ORDINANCE FOR SECTION 12.01.PDF</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-05/meeting-2938/28309/bie-proposed-charter-amendment-change-final-ordinance-for-section-12.01.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">BIE - PROPOSED CHARTER AMENDMENT CHANGE - FINAL ORDINANCE FOR SECTION 12.01.PDF</a></li>
</ul>
</div>
</article>
